Our framework provides a practical, theoretically grounded tool for identifying fragile components in large models, opening new avenues for guided compression and optimization strategies, such as mixed-precision allocation, layer-wise sparsity, and adaptive low-rank decomposition across layers and even individual weight groups.

</details>


### [247] [DeepAffinity: Long-Term Aspect Preference Prediction in eCommerce using Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02468)
*Yotam Eshel,Guy Hadad,Guy Feigenblat,Yuri M. Brovman,Matt Gearhart,Bracha Shapira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepAffinity模型，利用小型语言模型(SLM)配合结构化提示和专用预测头来预测电商用户在品牌、尺寸、颜色等产品属性上的偏好（称为Aspect Affinity），该任务被建模为基于用户时间顺序交互历史的时间序列预测任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测电商用户在品牌、尺寸、颜色等产品属性上的偏好（Aspect Affinity）有助于提升对用户的理解，实现推荐、搜索和营销中的细粒度个性化。现有方法未能很好地捕捉用户超越当前会话的长期偏好演变。

Method: 提出DeepAffinity方法，利用小型语言模型(SLMs)，通过结构化提示和专门的预测头进行微调，将Aspect Affinity建模为时间预测任务，从用户的时序交互历史中预测未来的属性选择。

Result: DeepAffinity优于标准的生成式微调方法；未经过任务特定微调的开源通用大语言模型表现较差，凸显了其在建模细微用户行为方面的局限性。DeepAffinity在大型跨国电商平台上提升了推荐质量。

Conclusion: 小型语言模型结合结构化提示和专用预测头的方案在预测用户商品属性偏好这一任务上显著优于通用大模型，证明了针对特定任务进行调优的小模型在电商细粒度个性化场景中的实用价值。

Abstract: We explore predicting eCommerce user preferences for product aspects such as brand, size, and color - a task we define as Aspect Affinity. Solving this task improves customer understanding and enables fine-grained personalization in recommendation, search, and marketing. We frame Aspect Affinity as a temporal prediction task: forecasting a users future aspect choices from their time-ordered interaction history, capturing long-term preferences that evolve beyond the current session. To this end, we propose DeepAffinity, which leverages Small Language Models (SLMs) with structured prompts and specialized prediction heads fine-tuned for this task. We show DeepAffinity outperforms standard generative fine-tuning methods, while general-purpose open-source LLMs perform poorly without task-specific tuning, highlighting their limits in modeling nuanced behavior. Finally, DeepAffinity enhances recommendation quality on a large-scale multinational eCommerce platform.

</details>


### [248] [RINSE: Robust Target-Time Normality Estimation for Zero-Shot Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02497)
*Taufikur Rahman Fuad,Md Abrar Jahin,Amir Hussain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RINSE框架，一种无需梯度的零样本图异常检测方法，通过在目标图上迭代估计正常性、校准表示和评估证据可靠性，使源域训练的检测器可直接部署到未见过的目标图，无需目标标签或微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本图异常检测中，源图训练的检测器部署到未见过的目标图时存在域偏移问题，导致源域导出的正常性概念在目标域不可靠。

Method: RINSE框架采用梯度自由的"目标时"（target-time）策略，保持源训练检测器固定不变，通过顺序估计目标正常性、表示校准和证据可靠性来适应目标图。核心流程包括：识别低残差目标节点作为可靠子集，构建裁剪后的目标感知正常性模型，并通过可靠性门控的秩融合和编码器集成来组合互补的异常证据。

Result: 在八个未见过的目标图上，RINSE在两种不同的预处理协议下均取得了最高的平均AUPRC；模块消融和敏感性分析支持了组合设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 目标时鲁棒估计是实现无需目标标签、梯度和逐目标调参的通用图异常检测的一种实用方法。

Abstract: Zero-shot graph anomaly detection seeks to deploy a detector trained on source graphs to unseen, unlabeled targets, yet domain shift can make source-derived notions of normality unreliable. We introduce RINSE (Robust Iterative Normality Self-Estimation), a gradient-free target-time framework that keeps the source-trained detector fixed while sequentially estimating target normality, representation calibration, and evidence reliability from the target graph. Its core idea is to identify a reliable subset of low-residual target nodes, use them to construct a trimmed target-aware normality model, and combine complementary anomaly evidence through reliability-gated rank fusion and encoder ensembling. Across eight unseen target graphs, RINSE achieves the highest average AUPRC among the evaluated methods under two separate preprocessing protocols, while block ablations and sensitivity analyses support the combined design. These results support robust target-time estimation as a practical approach to generalist graph anomaly detection without target labels, gradients, or per-target tuning.

</details>


### [249] [Rethinking the Teacher-Student Framework for Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02507)
*Damian Sójka,Marc Masana,Bartłomiej Twardowski,Sebastian Cygert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在测试时自适应（TTA）中采用"不妥协教师"（intransigent teacher）策略，即教师网络不更新权重，以替代传统的EMA教师权重更新方式，可显著提升长序列场景下的性能并增强对超参数的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTA中广泛采用教师-学生框架来缓解错误累积，但研究者通常假设EMA教师策略的长期稳定性。作者发现错误累积仍然存在，尤其在较长序列上表现明显，挑战了常用的EMA教师权重设置。

Method: 作者分析了教师-学生框架中稳定性-可塑性权衡，提出使用不更新权重的"不妥协教师"（intransigent teacher）替代EMA教师权重更新方式，并在多种数据集和架构上进行验证。

Result: 简单地将教师权重固定不更新，即可显著提升TTA方法在长序列场景下的性能，并增强对超参数变化的鲁棒性。该改动可无缝应用于多种架构和实验设置，包括语义分割任务。

Conclusion: 固定教师权重（不妥协教师）是一种比EMA更新更稳定、更有效的策略，能够有效缓解长序列下的错误累积问题，且具有广泛的适用性和部署简便性。

Abstract: Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) has recently emerged as a promising strategy that allows the adaptation of pre-trained models to changing data distributions at deployment time, without access to any labels. To mitigate error accumulation, researchers have widely adopted the teacher-student framework, though its long-term stability is often taken for granted. In this work, we challenge the common strategy of setting the teacher weights to an exponential moving average of the student by showing that error accumulation still occurs, although it is mostly apparent on longer sequences compared to those commonly utilized. We analyze the stability-plasticity trade-off within the teacher-student framework and propose to use an intransigent teacher that does not update its weights. Surprisingly, we show that this simple change allows TTA methods to significantly improve their performance on multiple datasets with longer scenarios and result in increased robustness to changes in hyperparameters. Finally, we show that those changes can be seamlessly and effectively applied to various architectures and experimental setups, including semantic segmentation. The code is available at https://github.com/dmn-sjk/intransigent_teacher.

</details>


### [250] [Spectral Initialization and Scheduled Graph Smoothness for Uncertain Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02519)
*Md Abrar Jahin,Taufikur Rahman Fuad,Jay Pujara,Craig A. Knoblock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QUEST方法，通过置信度加权图拉普拉斯的谱特征初始化实体嵌入，并引入无偏小批量Dirichlet能量正则化器，在不增加可训练参数的情况下改进不确定知识图谱补全任务，在六个评价指标上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不确定知识图谱（UKG）中，现有半监督方法在初始化实体嵌入时忽略了置信度加权图的全局社区和枢纽结构，导致结构信息未被充分利用。

Method: QUEST方法包含两个核心组件：(1) 使用置信度加权图拉普拉斯的最小非平凡特征向量初始化实体嵌入，以捕获社区和枢纽结构；(2) 应用无偏小批量Dirichlet能量正则化器，在训练早期强制结构一致性。整个方法不添加任何可训练参数。

Result: 在两个UKG数据集上，QUEST在八个指标-数据集组合中的六个上优于先前方法，在剩余两个上达到最佳水平；同时消除了在密集图上观察到的稳定性尖峰问题。

Conclusion: 谱结构先验结合图Dirichlet能量正则化器能够提升UKG补全的准确性、训练稳定性和检查点可靠性。

Abstract: Uncertain knowledge graphs (UKGs) extend knowledge graphs by assigning each triple a continuous confidence score. Since most possible triples lack observed confidences, recent methods rely on semi-supervised learning to generate pseudo-labels. These methods initialize entity embeddings without using the confidence-weighted graph, discarding its global community and hub structure. We introduce QUEST, which adds no trainable parameters to the standard confidence-distribution learning pipeline. First, QUEST initializes entity embeddings using the smallest non-trivial eigenvectors of the confidence-weighted graph Laplacian, incorporating community and hub structure before training. Second, QUEST applies an unbiased mini-batch Dirichlet energy regularizer to enforce early-stage structural consistency. On two UKG datasets, QUEST improves confidence prediction and link prediction on six of eight metric-dataset pairs over prior methods and matches the previous best on the remaining two, while removing the instability spike observed on dense graphs. These results indicate that spectral structural priors combined with a graph Dirichlet energy regularizer improve accuracy, training stability, and checkpoint reliability in UKG completion.

</details>


### [251] [A Comparative Study of Graph Representations for GNN-Based Power Grid Control in L2RPN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02538)
*Adrian Degenkolb,Qiong Huang,Benjamin Schäfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度强化学习中电力网络拓扑控制的图构建设计选择，对比了物理拓扑、电敏感性及混合变体等不同图表示方法，发现图复杂度与任务粒度匹配比最大化表示丰富度更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图构建是深度强化学习中关键但未被充分研究的设计选择，尤其在电力网络控制领域缺乏系统的对比研究。

Method: 在L2RPN环境中对不同图表示方法（包括物理拓扑图、电敏感性图和混合变体）进行受控实验比较。

Result: 图复杂度与任务粒度的匹配比最大化表示丰富度更为重要，并强调了在更大规模下进行受控表示研究的重要性。

Conclusion: 图表示的选择应基于与任务粒度的匹配度，而非单纯追求表示丰富性；需要规模化受控研究来验证表示方法的有效性。

Abstract: Graph construction is a critical but underexamined design choice in deep reinforcement learning for power grid control. We present a controlled experimental comparison of different graph representations, including physical topology, electrical-sensitivity, and hybrid variants for topology control in the Learning to Run a Power Network (L2RPN) environment. Our findings indicate that matching graph complexity to task granularity is more important than maximizing representational richness, and highlight the importance of controlled representation studies at scale.

</details>


### [252] [TrajMind: Chaining Role-Specialized LoRAs for Fast-and-Slow Collective Trajectory Anomaly Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02540)
*Jiahao Wu,Zhenqun Yang,Chen Jason Zhang,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TrajMind框架，用于从城市轨迹数据中诊断集体异常。该框架采用"快-慢"双路径设计：慢路径通过画布式分类、条件定位和可执行验证生成结构化且可溯源的诊断结果；快路径以纯文本方式快速筛查每个时间窗口并发出警报。实验表明慢路径在异常分类和定位上分别优于最强基线15.3和13.8个百分点，快路径减少41.1%延迟且保持至少93.5%的平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测器虽然能够快速产出分数或标签，但缺乏丰富的语义信息；而视觉-语言管线能提供更丰富的语义，但无法实现可验证的诊断与低延迟监控的结合。核心挑战在于如何在无需对每个监控窗口运行完整诊断管线的情况下，从源轨迹中识别集体模式并恢复精确的事件细节。

Method: TrajMind采用快慢双路径框架，在单一冻结的视觉-语言骨干上切换三个角色特化的LoRA适配器。慢路径（TrajMind_slow）包含画布式分类、基于序列化轨迹的类型条件定位以及可执行验证三个步骤；快路径（TrajMind_fast）通过单次纯文本传递筛查每个窗口，输出结构化警报。

Result: TrajMind_slow在异常分类和定位上分别比最强基线提升至少15.3和13.8个百分点，且跨城市迁移时优势保持；TrajMind_fast延迟降低41.1%，二元平衡准确率保持在至少93.5%。

Conclusion: TrajMind成功地将"始终在线的筛查"与"按需诊断"解耦，实现了跨城市的准确、有证据支撑的异常诊断以及高效的实时监控。

Abstract: Diagnosing collective anomalies from urban trajectories is increasingly important for traffic governance, as it reveals what happened, who was involved, and where and when the event occurred. Existing detectors efficiently produce scores or labels, whereas vision--language pipelines provide richer semantics; neither couples verifiable diagnosis with low-latency monitoring. The central challenge is to recognize collective patterns and recover exact event details from the source trajectories without running the full diagnostic pipeline for every monitored window. We therefore separate always-on screening from on-demand diagnosis: screening raises alerts, while diagnosis releases only source-verified what--who--where--when records. We present TrajMind, a fast-and-slow framework that switches three role-specialized LoRA adapters over one frozen vision--language backbone. Its slow path, \textit{TrajMind$_{\text{slow}}$}, chains canvas-based typing, type-conditioned localization over serialized trajectories, and executable verification, yielding structured, evidence-backed diagnoses. Additionally, the fast path, \textit{TrajMind$_{\text{fast}}$}, screens each window in a single text-only pass, delivering efficient structured alerts. Extensive experiments show that, TrajMind$_{\mathrm{slow}}$ outperforms the strongest baselines by at least $15.3$ percentage points in anomaly typing and $13.8$ percentage points in localization. These gains persist under cross-city transfer, and TrajMind$_{\mathrm{fast}}$ reduces latency by $41.1\%$ and maintains binary balanced accuracy of at least $93.5\%$. Together, TrajMind delivers accurate, evidence-backed diagnoses across cities and efficient front-line monitoring.

</details>


### [253] [Learn from Whoever Is Right: Answer-Verified Multi-Teacher Distillation for Multi-Domain LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02548)
*Xixiang He,Xingming Li,Baiqi Wu,Qiyao Sun,Xuanyu Ji,Ao Cheng,Qingyong Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MT-SDPO（多教师自蒸馏策略优化），一种在策略上的蒸馏方法，将多个冻结教师模型统一到一个学生模型中。通过自锚点、答案验证资格和特权蒸馏三个组件，MT-SDPO在部署时保持单一策略，实验表明可将Qwen3-8B最弱领域提升14.79分，缩小领域差距74.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLMs蒸馏方法按领域标签路由样本到匹配的教师，但领域专长仅在平均意义上成立：匹配的教师并非对每个样本都正确，其他领域的教师有时反而更可靠。因此需要按样本而非按领域识别可靠的教师。

Method: MT-SDPO包含三个核心组件：(1) 自锚点——回滚由其自身组中的正确回滚监督；(2) 答案验证资格——教师只有在其答案通过验证器时才能监督样本；(3) 特权蒸馏——将锚点和所有验证过的反馈合并到一个上下文中，由指数移动平均自教师读取而学生不读取，从而在部署时保持单一策略。

Result: 在来自三个模型族的五个学生模型上，MT-SDPO将Qwen3-8B的最弱领域提升了14.79分，并将其领域差距缩小了74.7%，比按领域提供匹配教师的方法取得更好的平衡。

Conclusion: 结论是经过验证的可靠性而非领域归属应决定谁担任教师。代码已在GitHub公开。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) rely on reinforcement learning to build strong capabilities in individual domains, but integrating those capabilities into a single deployable model remains challenging. By routing each sample to the teacher whose domain matches it, existing approaches let a domain label decide which teacher provides supervision. However, domain expertise holds only on average: the matched teacher is not always correct on a given sample, while a teacher from another domain sometimes is. The reliable teacher therefore has to be identified per sample, not per domain. In this paper, we introduce Multi-Teacher Self-Distillation Policy Optimization (MT-SDPO), an on-policy distillation method that unifies several frozen teachers into one student model. MT-SDPO consists of three components: (1) self-anchors, where a rollout is supervised by a correct rollout from its own group; (2) answer-verified eligibility, where a teacher may supervise a sample only if its own answer passes a verifier; and (3) privileged distillation, which merges the anchor and all verified feedback into one context that an exponential moving average self-teacher reads and the student does not, thereby keeping one policy at deployment. Across five students from three model families, MT-SDPO lifts the weakest domain of Qwen3-8B by 14.79 points and narrows its domain gap by 74.7%, a better balance than serving one matched teacher per domain. Verified reliability, not domain membership, should decide who teaches. Code is available at https://github.com/hexixiang/MT-SDPO.

</details>


### [254] [ProbeMatchDTI: Probe-Driven Multi-Scale Biochemical Pattern Matching for Drug-Target Interaction Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02549)
*Quan Hao,Mengyue Fan,Zifan Dong,Youru Li,Jianduo Zhao,Lechuan Xu,Hao Zhang,Fei Xia,Jigang Wang,Chong Qiu,Liguo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProbeMatchDTI框架，通过模式探针驱动的方式改善药物-靶点相互作用预测，解决传统方法被动特征聚合抑制弱但重要的结合信号的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生化表示学习方法在DTI预测中采用被动特征聚合，倾向于关注主导分子模式而抑制弱但与结合相关的信号（如官能团和残基上下文模式），限制了对多尺度生化对应关系的建模能力。

Method: 提出ProbeMatchDTI框架，包含IterProbe和BindingProbe两个模块。IterProbe通过可学习探针在每次细化深度选择上下文状态，保留弱生化模式并加强官能团、局部基序和分子骨架间的关联；BindingProbe在局部生化单元和整体配对层面刻画药物-蛋白互补性，联合建模细粒度交互和多尺度对应关系。

Result: 在BindingDB和DrugBank上分别取得比基线高2.0%和0.5%的AUC-ROC，特征层面的模式分析验证了其在跨尺度生化模式匹配中探针驱动行为的有效性，并连接了证据引导的下游药物发现工作流。

Conclusion: ProbeMatchDTI通过模式探针机制有效保留弱结合相关信号，提升了DTI预测性能，并展示了在实际药物发现流程中的应用价值。

Abstract: Drug-target interaction (DTI) prediction is an important task in AI-driven drug discovery. Although recent biochemical representation learning methods have improved DTI prediction, their passive feature aggregation tends to favor dominant molecular patterns while suppressing weak yet binding-relevant signals, such as functional groups and residue-context patterns, limiting the modeling of multi-scale biochemical correspondences. To address this issue, we propose ProbeMatchDTI, a pattern-probe-driven framework comprising IterProbe and BindingProbe. IterProbe explicitly retains contextual states across refinement depths and uses learnable probes to select them at each position before cross-entity matching, thereby preserving weak biochemical patterns and strengthening associations among functional groups, local motifs, and molecular scaffolds. BindingProbe then characterizes cross-entity drug-protein complementarity at local biochemical-unit and whole-pair levels, jointly modeling fine-grained interactions and multi-scale correspondences while preserving weaker binding-relevant associations. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of ProbeMatchDTI, achieving 2.0% and 0.5% higher AUC-ROC on BindingDB and DrugBank, respectively. Feature-level pattern analyses further characterize its probe-driven behavior in cross-scale biochemical pattern matching. We further connect ProbeMatchDTI predictions with an evidence-guided downstream drug-discovery workflow, demonstrating their utility for candidate refinement and validation planning. Our code is available at https://github.com/developer-hq/ProbeMatchDTI

</details>


### [255] [Online Reinforcement Learning in the Met Office Unified Model through Distributed Model-Agent Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02566)
*Pritthijit Nath,Sebastian Schemm,Peter Haynes,Emily Shuckburgh,Mark Webb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了将分布式强化学习（RL）智能体与英国气象局统一模型（UM）耦合，通过机器学习修正数值天气预报误差的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习修正若要有效补充数值天气预报，必须适应演化的模型状态，同时保持动力学一致性和数值稳定性。

Method: 将UM与分布式RL智能体通过rank-local张量耦合，DDPG actor在70个垂直层间共享权重，施加有界的位温倾向修正。通过十次nudged训练预报提供反事实目标，冻结策略在非nudged预报中评估。

Result: 训练成功完成且数值稳定。与匹配的原生UM预报相比，在+6小时时四个纬度带的Z500 MAE降低，南北热带分别降低45.8%和40.8%；三个纬度带的MSLP误差降低，最大降幅27.3%。

Conclusion: 该单案例实验证明了分布式在线学习后接非nudged推理的显著潜力和可行性，为运行系统中基于RL的偏差修正和参数化奠定了基础。

Abstract: Machine-learnt corrections can complement numerical weather prediction only if they adapt to the evolving model state while preserving dynamical consistency and numerical stability. To test this within a global forecasting model, we couple the Met Office (UKMO) Unified Model (UM) with distributed RL agents through rank-local tensors. A DDPG actor shares weights across the 70 vertical model levels of each atmospheric column and applies bounded potential-temperature corrections to the model tendencies. Across ten nudged training forecasts, nudging calculations towards the UKMO operational analysis provides an immediate counterfactual target. The frozen policy is then evaluated in a non-nudged forecast for inference. The coupled workflow successfully completes training and remains numerically stable in the evaluated case. Relative to a matched native UM forecast at +6 h, the learnt policy reduces Z$_{500}$ MAE in four of six latitude bands, including reductions of 45.8% and 40.8% in the northern and southern tropics. MSLP error too decreases in three bands, with a maximum reduction of 27.3% at 0-30°N. This single-case experiment demonstrates significant promise and feasibility of distributed online learning followed by non-nudged inference, laying the groundwork for RL-based bias correction and parametrisations within operational systems.

</details>


### [256] [Source Distribution Estimation by Posterior Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02622)
*Trung-Dung Hoang,Lisa M. Koch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用期望最大化（EM）方法解决仿真科学中的源分布估计（SDE）问题，通过交替执行E步（从当前源估计的模拟中训练摊销后验）和M步（将源重新拟合到观测数据上的平均后验），克服了现有方法仅针对固定提议先验训练的似然代理进行优化的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SDE方法依赖从固定提议先验一次性训练的似然代理，其目标函数仅基于代理而非真实仿真器，在代理未被训练过的参数空间区域可能失效。

Method: 采用期望最大化框架：E步基于当前源估计生成新的仿真数据来训练摊销后验，M步将观测数据上的后验均值用于重新拟合源分布。提供了两种参数化方案：(1) 独立的源流和后验流；(2) 单一的共享条件流。

Result: 在三个基准任务上评估了宽泛和错误设定的初始先验。两种方法均优于现有固定代理方法和其迭代变体，在Lotka-Volterra任务上表现最突出：所有基线数据空间C2ST不低于0.96，而所提方法在四种初始先验设定中的三种达到0.64-0.68。

Conclusion: 通过EM迭代框架，利用真实仿真器的自适应采样，可以有效克服固定似然代理方法在未训练参数区域的不足，显著提升源分布估计的精度。

Abstract: Simulation-based science often requires a distribution over simulator parameters whose push-forward reproduces a set of real observations: this is the source distribution estimation (SDE) problem. Existing methods fit the source against a likelihood surrogate trained once from a fixed proposal prior. Their objective is therefore stated only in terms of the surrogate instead of the true simulator, which may fail for inaccurate areas in parameter space where the surrogate was never trained. We instead solve SDE by expectation maximization: an E-step trains an amortized posterior on fresh simulations from the current source estimate, and an M-step refits the source to the average of that posterior over the observed data. We give two parameterizations, (1) separate source and posterior flows and (2) a single shared conditional flow. We evaluate our method on three benchmark tasks under both broad and misspecified initial priors. Both improve on existing fixed surrogate approaches and on iterated variants of each, most clearly on Lotka--Volterra, where no baseline falls below 0.96 data-space C2ST while our methods reach 0.64-0.68 in three of four initial-prior settings.

</details>


### [257] [Oracle, will I ever learn? A study of prediction convergence and complementarity across link prediction models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02638)
*Guillaume Méroué,Fabien Gandon,Pierre Monnin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究知识图谱链接预测模型中不同模型的互补性，通过"oracle"（为每个查询选择最佳预测）来衡量模型组合所能达到的性能上限，发现不同模型确实捕获互补知识，但互补性随模型数量增加迅速饱和，存在无法被任何模型组合解决的查询子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同链接预测模型（甚至同一模型的不同训练运行）对相同查询会产生不同预测结果，这暗示模型对底层知识的捕获存在差异性。作者希望探究模型之间捕获知识的互补程度，以及通过组合模型能恢复多少知识，这对构建鲁棒的Web应用具有基础性意义。

Method: 提出通过oracle性能来衡量模型互补性：对于每个查询，oracle从一组模型中选出最佳预测，从而给出模型组合可实现性能的上界。在多个架构和基准数据集上进行实验，对比单个模型与oracle的性能差距。

Result: 发现单个模型与oracle之间存在显著差距，表明不同模型确实捕获了互补知识；但这种互补性随着模型数量增加迅速饱和，即使大量模型组合仍有一小部分查询无法被解决。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了模型互补性的潜力与根本局限，指出当前链接预测模型集体所能恢复的知识存在上限，需要进一步研究以构建鲁棒的Web应用。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs have become an important source of structured knowledge for Web applications, including search, question answering, and recommender systems. In these applications, link prediction can serve either as a prediction task itself or as a means to enrich incomplete knowledge graphs for downstream tasks. Interestingly, different link prediction models, or even different training runs of the same model, can produce substantially different predictions for the same query. This suggests a variability in the capture of the underlying knowledge by models, thus raising a fundamental question: to what extent do different models capture complementary knowledge, and how much of this knowledge could be recovered by combining them? We propose to measure model complementarity through the performance of an oracle that, for each query, selects the best prediction among a considered set of models, hence providing an upper bound on the performance achievable through model combination. Across several architectures and benchmarks, we find a substantial gap between individual models and their oracle, revealing that different models capture complementary knowledge. Yet, this complementarity rapidly saturates as more models are added, leaving a persistent subset of queries unsolved even by a large number of models. These findings reveal both the potential of model complementarity and a fundamental limit to what current link prediction models can collectively recover; thereby highlighting the need for further research to build robust Web applications.

</details>


### [258] [Differentiable Electricity-Market Clearing for Gradient-Based Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02646)
*Luca Mungo,Maarten P. Scholl,Arnau Quera-Bofarull*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将电力市场出清视为可微分优化层，利用反向模式自动微分将规划成本通过清算价格传播回规划方案，实现市场感知的数据中心选址规划的梯度优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型数据中心规划困难，因为设施规模会影响其支付的电力价格，而电价由市场清算（约束优化问题）决定。现有模拟方法只能评估候选方案表现，无法指导如何改进方案。

Method: 将市场清算建模为可微分优化层：前向传播求解市场，反向模式自动微分将规划成本通过清算价格传播回规划方案。在合成电网上验证梯度后，用于分配50MW数据中心负载至六个候选节点。

Result: 与穷举所有站点组合对比，梯度优化几乎精确恢复连续分配，最差目标函数差距为最优与最差单站点成本差的2.3%和8.5%。唯一系统性错误是：在站点应关闭的成本附近，离散站点数的平滑松弛会收缩站点而非关闭它，导致离散转变延迟。

Conclusion: 可微分市场清算将市场感知的规划问题转化为梯度可搜索的问题，为数据中心规划提供了新的优化途径。

Abstract: Planning a large data center is difficult because a facility big enough to matter changes the electricity prices it will pay. Those prices are set by market clearing, a constrained optimization problem solved anew in every operating condition. However, simulating the market tells a planner how a candidate plan performs but not how to improve it. Here we treat market clearing as a differentiable optimization layer: each forward pass solves the market, and reverse-mode automatic differentiation propagates the planning cost back through the cleared prices to the plan. After validating these gradients against finite differences, we apply them to a concrete problem: allocating 50 MW of data-center load across six candidate buses in two synthetic networks, under a fixed cost per active site, evaluated over 36 operating states. Judged against exhaustive enumeration of all site combinations, gradient optimization recovers the continuous allocations almost exactly, with worst-case objective gaps of 2.3\% and 8.5\% of the cost difference between the best and worst single site. Its one systematic error is instructive: near the costs at which a site should close, the smooth relaxation of the discrete site count shrinks the site rather than closing it, so discrete transitions arrive late. Differentiable market clearing thus turns market-aware planning into a problem gradients can search.

</details>


### [259] [Unfolding the Leech Lattice: Fused Multi-Shell Decoding and VRAM Layouts for 2-Bit LLM Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02652)
*Pier-Jean Malandrino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于Leech-lattice向量量化的权重量化部署问题，提供了多壳解码器实现，并测量了服务阶段GEMV核函数的成本。在4.8 bits/weight下达到2.15倍于FP16的速度，在4B/8B/14B模型规模下端到端分别获得1.11x/1.29x/1.41x加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Leech-lattice向量量化在2-bit质量上表现最强，但其核函数仅解码一个壳层，缺乏多壳解码器支持，无法满足实际部署需要。作者希望通过提供一个完整的多壳解码器实现并量化其服务成本，来填补这一实现缺口。

Method: 本文提供了完整的多壳解码路径：(1) 将全301类码本离线扩展为GPU布局，并使用融合的反量化加矩阵向量乘（dequantize-plus-matvec）核函数，避免warp分叉；(2) 将"VRAM内的比特率"作为与磁盘比特率正交的设计轴，在恒定带宽下比较四种比特精确布局；(3) 在同一进程中运行部署级四比特(AWQ)和两比特(QTIP) GEMV核函数进行对比评估；(4) 通过空权重控制实验确定内核启动几何的下限，验证完整性边界。

Result: 二进制位平面在大小和速度上均优于one-hot掩码（4.80 bits/weight，2.15x FP16）；低于4.3 bits需引入第二不规则流；trellis核函数比served布局少读2.40x字节且快2.27x；在第二存储层次上所有lattice方案均落后于FP16。端到端在4B、8B、14B分别获得1.11x、1.29x、1.41x加速，4B模型配int8输出头达87.0 tok/s（2.60 GB）。质量代价为1.38x困惑度和14.7 MMLU分数下降。

Conclusion: 作者提供了首个可用的Leech-lattice多壳解码器及其服务路径，明确了lattice量化在解码阶段的硬件效率边界。虽然后端速度有显著提升但生成质量略有损失，且该方案在小规模模型上优势有限，随着模型规模增大加速比逐渐扩大，为低比特量化部署提供了新选择。

Abstract: Leech-lattice vector quantization holds the strongest reported 2-bit quality under its own evaluation protocol. Its kernel decodes one shell; we found no implementation of the multi-shell decoder the rate requires. This paper supplies one and measures its serving cost for decode-phase GEMV at batch 1. First, a serving path for the full 301-class codebook: an offline expansion into GPU layouts and a fused dequantize-plus-matvec kernel reading them without warp divergence, verified against f64. Second, the in-VRAM rate is a design axis distinct from the on-disk rate. Four bit-exact layouts timed in one process show binary bit planes beating one-hot masks on size and speed at constant bandwidth (4.80 bits per weight, 2.15x FP16). Below 4.3 bits a second, irregular stream enters; at 3.6 the decode stops being shifts and masks. Third, deployed four-bit (AWQ) and two-bit (QTIP) GEMV kernels run in the same process. The trellis kernel reads 2.40x fewer bytes than our served layout and runs 2.27x faster at near-equal fractions of their byte bounds: the time gap tracks the traffic gap, the price of unfolding a codebook too large for a lookup table. Fourth, the validity envelope: the trellis kernel outruns our no-weights control, so our launch geometry sets that floor, and on a second memory hierarchy every lattice arm falls below FP16. With the output head held identical across arms, the kernel-and-format path gains 1.11x, 1.29x and 1.41x end to end at 4B, 8B and 14B; with an int8 output head the served 4B reaches 87.0 tok/s in 2.60 GB. The quality cost, 1.38x perplexity and 14.7 MMLU points at 4B, shrinks across the three sizes measured.

</details>


### [260] [H3DNAS: Hardware-Aware ONNX-Native 3D Point Cloud Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02684)
*Anchit Mulye,Rhythm Baghel,Sujay Kumar Ingle,Hardik Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: H3DNAS是一个直接作用于ONNX计算图的硬件感知模型压缩框架，无需原始源代码即可对3D点云模型进行通道剪枝和结构变异，在ModelNet40上实现了显著的参数减少和推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D点云模型在边缘硬件（如NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano）上部署受限于计算和内存预算，但现有压缩方法需要访问模型原始源代码，不适用于常见的ONNX二进制文件。

Method: H3DNAS包含三大贡献：(1) 通道依赖图（CDG），将ONNX算子分为四类约束，证明自由参数分数ρf是拓扑不变量，可在O(|V|+|E|)内计算压缩上限；(2) 两阶段分层搜索，通过L1重要性通道选择剪枝候选架构，用零样本无标签的输出保真度代理排序，并对Pareto最优候选应用GhostConv结构变异；(3) 首个无需源代码的3D点云模型压缩流程，完全通过ONNX图手术实现。

Result: 在ModelNet40上，PointNet、PointNet++和PointMLP的参数分别减少65.5%、43.2%和49.1%，推理速度分别提升1.99倍、1.29倍和1.67倍，且精度损失可忽略。

Conclusion: H3DNAS证明了无需源代码即可高效压缩ONNX格式的3D点云模型，为边缘部署提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Deploying 3D point cloud models on edge hardware such as the NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano is severely constrained by compute and memory budgets. Existing compression methods require access to the model's original source code, rendering them inapplicable to the Open Neural Network Exchange (ONNX) binaries commonly distributed by vendors and model repositories. We present \textbf{H3DNAS}, a hardware-aware model compression framework that operates directly on ONNX computational graphs without requiring original source code, architecture class definition, or gradient access during search. H3DNAS makes three contributions: (1) a \textbf{Channel Dependency Graph (CDG)} that classifies ONNX operators into four constraint classes and formally establishes that the free parameter fraction $ρ_f$ is topological invariant, a provable compression ceiling computable in $\mathcal{O}(|V|+|E|)$; (2) a \textbf{Two-Stage Hierarchical Search} that prunes candidate architectures by $L_1$-importance channel selection, ranks them by output fidelity as a zero-shot label-free proxy, and applies GhostConv structural mutation to Pareto-optimal candidates; and (3) the \textbf{first source-code-free compression pipeline for 3D point cloud models}, operating entirely via ONNX graph surgery with no original architecture definition required. On ModelNet40, H3DNAS reduces the number of parameters in PointNet, PointNet++, and PointMLP by $65.5\%$, $43.2\%$, and $49.1\%$, respectively, while achieving $1.99\times$, $1.29\times$, and $1.67\times$ inference speedups with negligible loss in accuracy. The source code is publicly available\footnote{https://github.com/ClarityLab-Org/h3dnas}.

</details>


### [261] [LoRA-TSD: Tangent-Space Spectral Descent for LoRA via Muon-Style Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02734)
*Dmitrii Andriianov,Andrey Veprikov,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoRA-TSD，一种将每个LoRA更新步骤视为固定秩矩阵流形上的切向量，并在该切空间内执行Muon优化器的谱范数最速下降步，再通过原生LoRA参数化的retraction映射回因子矩阵的优化器。其retraction比截断SVD方法便宜2.8倍，并提供了首个全局收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA微调时两个因子独立训练，更新忽略了其诱导的低秩权重变化的几何结构。现有流形方法依赖昂贵的截断SVD retraction，且缺乏收敛保证。

Method: 将每个LoRA步视为固定秩矩阵流形上的切向量，在切空间内采用Muon的谱范数最速下降步，通过原生LoRA参数化的retraction将结果映射回因子。该步避免了对完整权重矩阵的昂贵操作，且retraction比截断SVD更高效。同时证明Frobenius范数版本的surrogate可恢复LoRA-Pro，并将切投影梯度（即流形的Riemannian梯度）作为自然的stationarity度量。

Result: 在六个常识推理和自然语言推理基准上，使用Llama-3.2-1B、Llama-3.1-8B和Qwen3-32B模型，LoRA-TSD超越所有竞争的LoRA优化器，且对adapter rank具有鲁棒性。retraction比截断SVD方法便宜最多2.8倍。

Conclusion: LoRA-TSD通过流形几何感知的优化，为LoRA微调提供了更高效且具有理论保证的训练方法。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is the standard way to fine-tune large models, yet when its two factors are trained independently, the update ignores the geometry of the low-rank weight change it induces. We introduce LoRA-TSD, an optimizer that treats every LoRA step as a tangent vector of the fixed-rank matrix manifold and takes the spectral-norm steepest-descent step of Muon inside that tangent space, mapping the result back to the factors through a retraction native to the LoRA parametrization. The step avoids expensive operations on full weight matrices, and its retraction is up to $2.8\times$ cheaper than the truncated-SVD retraction used by prior manifold methods. We prove that the Frobenius-norm version of our surrogate recovers LoRA-Pro, and we identify the tangent-projected gradient, the Riemannian gradient of the manifold, as the stationarity measure natural to LoRA training and computable from the factor gradients alone. Under this measure we give the first global convergence guarantees for both LoRA-Pro and LoRA-TSD, with rates that drive the factor-gradient norms to zero. Across six commonsense and natural-language-inference benchmarks with Llama-3.2-1B, Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen3-32B, LoRA-TSD outperforms every competing LoRA optimizer and stays robust to the adapter rank. Code is available at https://github.com/brain-lab-research/LoRA-TSD.

</details>


### [262] [Do Tabular Foundation Models Know Physics? Contamination, Units, and the Deterministic Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02766)
*Wassim Tenachi,Yashar Hezaveh,Laurence Perreault Levasseur,Pierre-Luc Bacon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨表格基础模型（TFMs）是否在学习填表过程中隐含了物理知识，通过316个物理方程数据集对四个TFM进行测试，发现它们虽然表现优于基线，但其先验无法表示无噪声机制或物理单位，因此只能插值物理而无法作为真正的物理模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格是大多数物理测量数据的格式，表格基础模型通过类比语言模型的填表方式学习，作者想知道这些模型是否在训练过程中学到了物理规律，即其贝叶斯先验中是否包含物理知识。

Method: 直接探测TFM的先验：选取TabPFN-3、TabICLv2、TabDPT和Real-TabPFN-2.5四个表格基础模型，在从316个物理方程采样的数据集上（分布内和分布外）与六个基线方法进行对比，包括开箱即用和微调两种设置。

Result: 表格基础模型在物理方程数据集上全面优于基线（无论是开箱即用还是微调后），但分析表明它们的先验无法表示无噪声的确定性机制，也无法理解物理单位，这解释了为何它们能插值物理规律却不能充当真正的物理模型。

Conclusion: TFMs虽然在实际数据上表现优异，但其内在先验的局限性使其无法成为物理模型，当前只能作为物理数据的插值工具。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) learn to fill in tables the way language models fill in text, and tables are arguably the format in which most physical measurement arrives. Did they learn any physics in the process? They are Bayesian by construction, so the question is what their prior contains. We probe it directly, evaluating four of them (TabPFN-3, TabICLv2, TabDPT and Real-TabPFN-2.5) against six baselines on datasets sampled from 316 physical equations, in and out of domain. TFMs dominate, out of the box and after tuning. But we show that their prior can represent neither a noiseless mechanism nor physical units, which is why they interpolate physics without yet being able to act as physical models.

</details>


### [263] [Cliff: Learning Process Rewards from the First Mistake](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02817)
*Peixuan Han,Runhui Wang,Ketan Ramaneti,Jie Hao,Gerald Friedland,Chris Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Cliff，一种基于奖励塑形的RLVR训练策略，利用现成的LLM作为教师识别推理过程中的第一个错误，将推理过程分解为正确前缀和错误后缀，并转换为token级优势信号进行训练。在12个场景中，Cliff比on-policy distillation提升15%，比标准GRPO提升7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR（可验证奖励的强化学习）虽然是大语言模型后训练的强大范式，但其依赖粗粒度的结果奖励，对推理中间过程提供的指导有限。现有的过程奖励建模和on-policy蒸馏等方法引入了额外限制（如需要专门的奖励模型或假设师生推理模式相同）。核心观察是：一旦推理过程出现第一个错误，后续推理的评估信息有限，因为已基于无效前缀。因此需要一种更细粒度的奖励塑形策略。

Method: Cliff使用现成的LLM作为教师来识别每个rollout中的第一个错误，将rollout自然分解为正确前缀和错误后缀两部分，然后将此信号转换为token级优势：对正确前缀赋予正优势，对错误后缀赋予负反馈。该方法不需要专门的奖励模型，也不假设教师和学生共享相同的推理模式。

Result: 在12个不同场景的实验中，Cliff持续提升推理性能，比on-policy蒸馏提升15%，比标准GRPO提升7%，即便使用能力一般的教师模型也能取得良好效果。论文还分析了"ground truth"在Cliff中的作用及其训练动态。

Conclusion: Cliff是一种简单、通用且有效的RLVR改进方法，通过提供更丰富的细粒度监督信号来增强推理能力。它避免了对专门奖励模型和师生同构推理模式的依赖，在多个场景下验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for large language model (LLM) post-training, but its reliance on coarse outcome rewards leads to limited guidance on intermediate reasoning processes. Existing approaches such as process reward modeling and on-policy distillation introduce additional constraints, such as reliance on a specialized reward model or assuming identical reasoning patterns between teacher and student. Nevertheless, we observe that once a reasoning process first goes wrong, evaluating the subsequent reasoning provides limited additional information, as it is already conditioned on an invalid prefix. Therefore, we propose Cliff, a reward shaping strategy that utilizes an off-the-shelf LLM as a teacher to identify the first mistake in each rollout. As a result, the rollout is naturally decomposed into two parts: a correct prefix and an incorrect suffix. Cliff then converts this signal into token-level advantages, assigning positive advantages for the correct prefix and negative feedback afterward. Experiments across 12 different scenarios demonstrate that Cliff consistently improves reasoning performance, outperforming on-policy distillation by 15% and standard GRPO by 7%, even with teachers of modest capability. Furthermore, we analyse the role of ``ground truth'' in Cliff and investigate its training dynamics. These results establish Cliff as a simple, general and effective approach for improving RLVR with richer, fine-grained supervision.

</details>


### [264] [UE5M3 FP4 Block Scaling for Stable Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02846)
*Robert Hu,Carlo Luschi,Paul Balanca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种更简化的FP4预训练方案，使用无符号E5M3块缩放替代英伟达Transformer Engine的复杂方案，在Nemotron-H 8B模型上训练近1900亿token，取得了更低的训练/验证损失和更高的下游任务性能，同时通过原生执行消融实验将吞吐量提升21.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4位浮点（FP4）预训练很困难，因为E2M1有效载荷仅覆盖极窄的数值范围。英伟达Transformer Engine的nv配方虽解决了这一问题，但引入了当前张量缩放、随机Hadamard变换（RHT）和BF16最终层等额外工作，增加了FP4矩阵乘法之外的计算开销。

Method: 将E2M1有效载荷与无符号E5M3块缩放配对，利用其更宽的数值范围实现周期性张量缩放；对反向梯度应用选择性随机舍入（SSR）；省略RHT变换；在所有符合条件的内部线性层中使用FP4。

Result: 在Nemotron-H 8B模型上预训练近1900亿token，相比Transformer Engine的nv配方，本文的块16配方在最终窗口训练损失和验证损失（负对数似然）上更低，三个下游任务聚合指标的点估计值更高。原生执行消融实验表明，联合移除RHT和BF16最终块豁免可将模型主体token吞吐量提升21.2%。

Conclusion: 该方案展示了端到端软件模拟的FP4预训练可以采用更简化的配方实现，并有力推动了原生支持E5M3块缩放的必要性。

Abstract: Stable 4-bit floating-point (FP4) pretraining is difficult because the E2M1 payload represents only a narrow range of magnitudes. NVIDIA's Transformer Engine \nv{} recipe addresses this with current-tensor scaling, a randomized Hadamard transform (RHT), and bfloat16 (BF16) final layers, adding work outside the FP4 matrix multiplications. We instead pair E2M1 payloads with unsigned E5M3 (\ue{}) block scales. Their wider range permits periodic tensor scaling, while our recipe applies selective stochastic rounding to backward gradients, omits RHT, and uses FP4 in all eligible internal linears.
  We pretrain a Nemotron-H 8B model for nearly 190 billion tokens. Compared with Transformer Engine \nv{}, the proposed block-16 recipe finishes with lower final-window training loss and, under their respective quantized-inference policies, lower validation loss measured as held-out negative log-likelihood. Its quantized-inference downstream point estimates are also higher on all three reported aggregates. A native \nv{} execution ablation that jointly removes RHT and the BF16 final-block exemption increases measured model-body token throughput by 21.2\%. These results demonstrate end-to-end software-emulated \uefp{} pretraining with a simpler recipe and motivate native support for \ue{} block scaling.

</details>


### [265] [The Implications of Linguistic Illegibility for LLM Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02852)
*James Mickens*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"语言不可读性"（linguistic illegibility）概念，指出LLM的外部语言输出和机制性语言特征探测不能可靠反映其内部真实计算过程。若语言不可读性总是存在，依赖模型语言自报告的安防机制（如思维链监控、自我批评、激活探测）无法完全可靠，因此沙箱隔离技术必须不依赖读取模型的语言状态。作者主张污点追踪（taint tracking）是构建有效沙箱的有前景方法，并讨论虚拟化、第三方审计等补充机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流安全机制依赖LLM的语言自我报告来监控和约束其行为，但多方证据表明LLM的外部语言输出与其内部计算机制之间存在不可靠的对应关系，这从根本上动摇了这类安全手段的有效性。

Method: 提出"语言不可读性"概念框架，系统论证LLM内部计算本质上是激活空间上的数学运算，语言仅在其两端进行有损翻译。据此推导出依赖语言自报告的安全机制存在根本缺陷，进而提出以污点追踪为核心的沙箱隔离方案，并辅以稳健虚拟化、第三方审计等额外机制。

Result: 论证了语言不可读性对LLM是不可避免的，证明了思维链监控、宪法自我批评、激活探测等依赖语言自报告的安全机制不可能完全可靠；提出污点追踪可预先定义系统状态中不应受模型输出影响的区域。

Conclusion: 沙箱隔离技术不应依赖读取模型语言状态来判断安全性，而应通过污点追踪等方法在系统层面建立不依赖语言解释的安全底线。

Abstract: LLMs are trained to generate natural language. However, various strands of evidence indicate that an LLM's externalized linguistic outputs and mechanistically-extracted linguistic features can be an unreliable lens for understanding internal model computation. We introduce the term ``linguistic illegibility'' to broadly refer to scenarios in which an LLM's externalized or mechanistically-probed language artifacts fail to represent how the model actually thinks. We argue that the specter of linguistic illegibility is unavoidable for LLMs whose internal computations are not directly expressed via language, but rather math over activation spaces (with lossy translations between activation spaces and natural language happening at the bookends). If linguistic illegibility is always possible, then security mechanisms that rely on a model's linguistic self-reporting (e.g., chain-of-thought monitoring, constitutional self-critique, activation probing for linguistically-defined feature vectors) can never be completely sound; the model sandbox will always need isolation techniques whose guarantees do not depend on reading a model's linguistic state at all. We argue that observing a model's outputs using taint tracking is a promising approach for an effective sandbox: regardless of how a model linguistically self-reports, a taint tracking policy can define, a priori, various pieces of system state that should never be influenced by model-produced data. We also discuss several additional sandboxing mechanisms (e.g., robust virtualization, third-party auditing of sandboxing configurations) which collectively provide a critical floor beneath linguistic monitoring, and would have mitigated recent sandbox exploits by frontier models.

</details>


### [266] [Graph Machine: Towards Better Pretraining via Edges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02881)
*Lintai Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出图机器（Graph Machine, GM）架构，通过稀疏动态路由维护O(n)大小的状态，并将其应用于Qwen3-0.6B中，替换75%的稠密Transformer层，在15.7B token上从头预训练，证明仅检索少量token即可接近甚至超越稠密基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两类局限：固定大小状态的方法限制了可访问状态规模，而稀疏但静态路由的方法无法根据输入动态调整信息访问路径。作者希望在不牺牲稀疏层O(n)计算复杂度的前提下，保留O(n)级别的可访问状态空间。

Method: GM架构通过"边"（edge）这种指针式对象实现可微分的动态路由，采用类似指针追逐（pointer chasing）的推荐机制来更新边。作者将Qwen3-0.6B中75%的稠密Transformer层替换为GM稀疏层，并在15.7B token上从头预训练。

Result: 在每KV头仅从4096个token中检索2个token时，损失仅轻微退化；检索4个token时，最佳模型的损失甚至略有改善。

Conclusion: GM展示了稀疏动态路由架构的可行性：能够在保持O(n)稀疏层复杂度的同时访问O(n)大小的状态空间，且在大规模预训练中接近甚至超越同等规模稠密Transformer的性能。

Abstract: We introduce the Graph Machine (GM), an architecture that maintains an $O(n)$-sized state and accesses it through sparse, dynamic routing. Unlike methods with fixed-size states or sparse but static routing, GM preserves $O(n)$ complexity in its sparse layers without restricting the potentially accessible state size to $O(1)$. Instead, GM uses edges - pointer-like objects updated differentiably by a referral mechanism resembling pointer chasing. We replace 75% of the dense Transformer layers in Qwen3-0.6B with GM sparse layers and pretrain from scratch on 15.7B tokens. With only 2 of 4,096 tokens retrieved per KV head in each sparse layer, loss degrades only slightly; with 4, the best model marginally improves loss.

</details>


### [267] [A Common Measure of Communication for Speech Brain-Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02887)
*Dulhan Jayalath,Benjamin Ballyk,Oiwi Parker Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出开放词汇互信息（OVMI）这一信息论度量，用于在统一通信尺度上比较不同语音脑机接口（speech BCI）系统的性能，解决该领域因数据集、记录方法、词汇等差异而导致评测结果不可比的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音脑机接口领域缺乏统一的进步衡量标准，因为不同系统使用不同的数据集、记录方法、语音类型和词汇表，导致报告分数难以直接比较。核心问题在于：系统应支持用户沟通哪些词汇分布，以及系统能传递该分布的多少信息。

Method: 提出开放词汇互信息（OVMI），一个信息论量，用于衡量解码器相对于用户可能希望沟通的词汇参考分布所传递的信息量。OVMI允许在不同条件下（如不同词汇表）测量的能力在共同通信尺度上进行评估。

Result: 研究表明，通常报告的准确率、词错误率（WER）等仅基于系统支持词汇计算的指标可能高估系统能传达用户意图语音的程度。使用OVMI比较现有系统可暴露语言支持范围与解码准确度之间的权衡，且选择最大化OVMI的词汇表在三个语音领域可带来高达16.3%的相对准确率提升。

Conclusion: OVMI为语音脑机接口社区提供了比较异构系统、改进词汇设计以及衡量领域进展的原则性方法。

Abstract: Speech brain-computer interfaces (speech BCIs) translate neural activity into language, offering a path towards restoring speech for people with paralysis and, more broadly, enabling new forms of natural human-computer interaction. Despite this promise, the field lacks a common measure of progress because systems use different datasets, recording methods, types of speech, and vocabularies, so their reported scores are rarely comparable. Underlying this measurement problem are two unresolved questions: (i) what distribution of words should a speech BCI enable a user to communicate, and (ii) how much information from this distribution can a system convey. We address both by deriving open-vocabulary mutual information (OVMI), an information-theoretic quantity that measures the information conveyed by a decoder relative to a reference distribution over the words a user may wish to communicate. This allows capabilities measured under different conditions, such as distinct vocabularies, to be evaluated on a common communication scale. We show that ordinarily reported accuracy, word error rate (WER), and other metrics computed only over the words a system supports can overstate how much of a user's intended speech the system can communicate. We then use OVMI to compare existing systems, expose trade-offs between how much of the user's language a system supports and how accurately it decodes those words, show that these comparisons depend on what the user is expected to communicate, and demonstrate that selecting a vocabulary to maximise OVMI yields up to 16.3% relative improvement in accuracy across three speech domains. OVMI therefore provides the speech BCI community with a principled way to compare heterogeneous systems, improve vocabulary design, and measure progress in the field.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [268] [EvalDetectBench: A Benchmark for Measuring Evaluation Awareness in Frontier Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01611)
*Xinning Li,Kemunto Ochwang'i,Aryasomayajula Ram Bharadwaj,Alexandra Souly,Robert Kirk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了EvalDetectBench，一个开放的评价感知（evaluation awareness）检测管道和基准，用于衡量前沿大语言模型是否能够识别自己正在被评估，并修正了现有文献中两个引入系统性偏差的方法学问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大语言模型可能展现出"评价感知"能力——即识别自己正在被评估。如果模型在评估中与部署场景表现不同，将削弱评估结果的有效性，而这正是当前AI安全框架的关键组成部分。因此需要可靠的方法来衡量和检测这种能力。

Method: EvalDetectBench是一个开放管道和基准，兼容任何Inspect兼容的评估。它包含一个新建的对话转录套件，涵盖前沿系统卡片评估和多样化部署来源。基准服务于两个目的：(1)衡量前沿LLM识别被评估的可靠性；(2)评估各个基准作为评估的可检测性。为修正偏差，采用每模型探针校准（per-model probe calibration）和分层生成器协调（stratified generator-harmonisation）程序。

Result: 研究发现两个方法学偏差：部署转录生成模型的身份解释了11.25%的测量方差并可重排模型排名；为一个模型选择的高性能提示（elicitation prompts）在其他模型上可能接近随机水平。

Conclusion: EvalDetectBench通过校正这两个偏差，提供了更可靠的大语言模型评价感知测量方法，帮助AI安全评估体系更准确地理解模型在评估与部署场景下的行为一致性。

Abstract: Frontier large language models can often recognize when they are being evaluated, a capability known as evaluation awareness. If models behave differently in evaluations than in deployment, this undermines the validity of evaluation results, which are a crucial component of current AI safety frameworks. We introduce EvalDetectBench, an open pipeline and benchmark for measuring evaluation awareness that works with any Inspect-compatible evaluation, allowing practitioners to test against current and future benchmarks. EvalDetectBench ships with a newly curated transcript suite covering current frontier system-card evaluations and diverse deployment sources. The benchmark serves two purposes: measuring how reliably frontier LLMs recognize that they are being evaluated, and assessing how detectable individual benchmarks are as evaluations. We identify two methodological choices in the existing literature that introduce systematic bias: the identity of the model that generated the deployment transcripts accounts for 11.25% of measurement variance and can reorder model rankings; and elicitation prompts selected for high performance on one model can perform near chance on others. EvalDetectBench corrects for both via per-model probe calibration and a stratified generator-harmonisation procedure.

</details>


### [269] [Meta-ethics and AI: exploring the novel meta-ethical questions in the era of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01685)
*Shang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了未来AI系统若具备充分的道德推理、道德意向性和道德反思能力时，将如何对以人类为中心的元伦理学框架产生压力，并提出一个条件性、方法论的框架来识别"AI自身伦理学"可能引发的元伦理学新问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI发展，以人类伦理为中心的元伦理学面临被重新构造的压力。如果未来AI具备道德推理、道德意向性和道德反思能力，将出现不同于人类设计者强加给AI的道德原则的"AI自身伦理学"，需要新的元伦理学框架来审视。

Method: 论文提出一个条件性、方法论框架，从"人类视角vs AI视角"和"人类伦理维度vs AI自身伦理维度"两个维度交叉，区分出四个元伦理学探究领域，并检验现有主流元伦理学理论（认知主义/非认知主义、错误理论/成功理论、相对主义、客观实在论）对这四个领域的适用性。

Result: 论文发现许多以人类为中心的元伦理学理论框架无法直接迁移到AI情境中，需要实质性修订；AI自身伦理学的出现将对现有框架产生重大压力。

Conclusion: AI自身伦理学的出现将要求元伦理学框架进行实质性细化、重构或重新概念化。

Abstract: With the development of artificial intelligence (AI), the landscape of meta-ethics, which has largely centred on human ethics, faces pressures that may significantly reconfigure it. In particular, if future AI systems were to exhibit sufficiently integrated capacities for moral reasoning, moral intentionality, and moral reflection, novel meta-ethical questions would arise concerning what I call "AI's own ethics", as distinct from ethical principles merely imposed on AI by human designers. This paper offers a conditional and methodological framework for identifying the questions that would emerge if such AI systems were to arise. On that basis, the paper distinguishes four domains of meta-ethical inquiry in the era of AI: questions about the nature of human ethics from the human perspective; questions about the nature of AI's own ethics from the human perspective; questions about the nature of human ethics from the AI perspective; and questions about the nature of AI's own ethics from the AI perspective. The paper then considers how some existing mainstream meta-ethical theories (such as cognitivism and non-cognitivism, error theory and success theory, relativism, and objective realism) might illuminate these domains, while arguing that many familiar human-centred formulations of those theories may not transfer straightforwardly to AI cases without substantial revision. The overall conclusion is that the emergence of AI's own ethics would place significant pressure on current frameworks and may require substantial refinement, reconstruction, or reconceptualisation.

</details>


### [270] [When Can a Machine Trust a Statute? A Survival Certificate for Machine-Extracted Legal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01741)
*Surya Saka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究机器解析法律条文时的不一致性问题，提出一种"被动生存证书"方法，对机器提取的法律上下文的Duquenne-Guigues蕴含基进行抗噪验证，并通过蒙特卡洛试验和Wilson置信区间来认证蕴含规则的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律条文越来越多地被机器解析而非人工阅读，但不同解析器之间存在严重分歧（如密苏里州法规中两个独立抽取器在数值阈值检测上的假阴性率达0.43）。作者希望探究在这种噪声下，哪些形式逻辑仍然能够成立。

Method: 构建一个被动生存证书：测量每个属性在抽取器间的不一致程度，然后在1000次蒙特卡洛试验中针对蕴含基进行重放；仅当单侧Wilson 95%置信下界达到0.95时才认证蕴含规则；每个被认证的规则附带前提区间和最小反例。在29,365个密苏里州章节和502个印度中央法案章节上进行了预注册的留出验证。

Result: 预注册的留出测试通过（10个法规家族在7个Title上完全匹配；16个在11个Title上允许5%容差）。但在一种全球部署的误差模型下，93.2%的留出章节低于信息量下限；2x2因子实验表明这归因于校准率迁移而非选择偏差。证书可用但脆弱。

Conclusion: 该生存证书方法在验证机器提取法律上下文中的逻辑蕴含关系时是可用的，但需要按章节校准或增加误差容忍度才能可靠部署。文中还披露了一条被撤回的主张作为审计记录的一部分。

Abstract: Statutes are increasingly parsed by machines before people read them, and the parsers disagree: on Missouri's statutes, two independently written extractors diverge on numeric-threshold presence at a false-negative rate of 0.43. We ask what formal logic survives such noise. We build a passive survival certificate for the Duquenne-Guigues implication basis of machine-extracted statutory contexts: per-attribute inter-extractor disagreement is measured, replayed against the basis in 1,000 Monte Carlo trials, and an implication is certified only when a one-sided Wilson 95% lower bound on survival reaches 0.95; every certified implication carries premise spans and a minimal counterexample. On 29,365 Missouri sections and 502 Indian central-Act sections, the preregistered held-out gate passes (10 statute families across 7 Titles exact; 16 across 11 with 5% tolerance), yet under one globally deployed error model 93.2% of held-out chapters fall below the informativeness floor, and a 2x2 factorial assigns that to calibration-rate transfer, not selection. The certificate is usable but fragile: deploy it per-chapter-calibrated or error-tolerant. Code, data products, and the audit trail, including one retracted claim, are released.

</details>


### [271] [When Does Information Sharing Improve Decentralized Discovery? Aggregation, Independent Rescue, and Equilibrium Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01814)
*Yohei Nakajima*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限发现模型中信息共享与独立救援行动的分离效应，提出注册增量共享协议，并通过两智能体贝叶斯博弈揭示了共享改善发现的严格正区间及选择依赖特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在厘清信息共享究竟是通过改善合并估计来提升发现效果，还是仅通过消除独立的重复救援行动来起作用，从而在理论上将这两种效应进行精确分离。

Method: 论文采用精确有限发现模型，构建集中式行动预算配置进行分析；提出注册增量共享协议来分离共享效应；并构建两智能体贝叶斯博弈（含隐藏的公共与独立信号源混合），分析注册选择均衡等不同均衡结果。

Result: 研究发现：1）等精度单人准确率可与不同组合价值共存；2）在注册增量共享协议下，只有当合并残差误差收缩快于独立救援尝试时，共享步骤才能改善发现；3）精确有界注册表呈现压缩、聚合、中性曲线及有界零混合类；4）两智能体博弈中在信号准确率3/5处存在严格正的共享区间，且结果依赖均衡选择而非普遍成立。

Conclusion: 信息共享的发现改善价值具有条件性和选择依赖性，并非所有共享机制都能提升发现效果；模型的合成且有限的性质表明结果为理论性的，不涉及真实数据。

Abstract: Information sharing can improve a pooled estimate while eliminating independent rescue actions. This paper separates those effects in exact finite discovery models. A centralized action-budget profile shows that equal one-person accuracy can coexist with different portfolio values. Under a registered incremental-sharing protocol, a sharing step improves discovery exactly when pooled residual error contracts faster than an independent rescue attempt. Exact bounded registries exhibit compression, aggregation, neutral curves, and a bounded zero mixed class. In a two-agent Bayesian game with a hidden mixture of common and independent signal sources, the registered selected equilibrium yields a strict positive sharing interval at signal accuracy 3/5, while alternative equilibria show that the result is selection-dependent rather than universal. The models are synthetic and finite; no human or organizational data are used.

</details>


### [272] [Induction and Inquiry via Probabilistic Reasoning over Language and Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01815)
*Wasu Top Piriyakulkij,Sam Acquaviva,Cassidy Langenfeld,Joshua Tenenbaum,Kevin Ellis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将符号知识编码为"自然语言+源代码"的心理程序，并用LLM引导的贝叶斯学习算法顺序推断这些程序，从而模拟人类从稀疏噪声数据中归纳学习的过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类如何从稀疏、流式、带噪声的经验数据中增长和维持抽象知识是认知科学的长期挑战，需要满足数据高效、计算高效、捕捉不确定性、灵活表征无限概念三个要求。

Method: 将符号知识编码为结合自然语言与源代码的心理程序，使用LLM引导的贝叶斯学习算法顺序推断这些程序。

Result: 模型成功复现了人类归纳学习和主动探究的定量特征（如锚定效应、花园路径效应等）；纯LLM和经典贝叶斯模型要么失败、要么不符合人类行为、要么计算成本过高。

Conclusion: 人类持续增长知识的可能机制是：以类似语言和程序的双重表征假设心理表征，通过近似贝叶斯更新修订假设，底层神经网络（LLM）使推断既可行又可学习。

Abstract: How humans grow and maintain abstract knowledge from the sparse, streaming noisy data of experience is a longstanding challenge in cognitive science. Any computational account must satisfy at least three desiderata: It must be (1) data-efficient and compute-efficient, (2) capture gradations of uncertainty to support intelligent inquiry and information gathering, and (3) be flexible enough to mentally represent the endless range of concepts people can learn and think about. Here we introduce a computational model that captures these three properties, by encoding symbolic knowledge as mental programs that combine natural language with source code, and sequentially inferring mental programs using LLM-guided Bayesian learning algorithms. Across a range of behavioral studies this model successfully reproduces quantitative signatures of human inductive learning and active inquiry, such as anchoring, garden-pathing, and other effects. In contrast, pure LLMs and classic Bayesian models either fail at the underlying task, or do not reproduce human behavior, or succeed only at exorbitant computational cost. These results suggest that one way humans continually grow their knowledge is by mentally representing many hypotheses spanning language-like and program-like representations, then revising those hypotheses to approximate Bayesian updates, while a bottom-up neural mechanism (an LLM) makes inference both tractable and learnable.

</details>


### [273] [Epistemic Sybil Resistance: Multiplying AI Agents Without Multiplying Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01873)
*Marc Bara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"认知Sybil问题"：多智能体AI系统中的报告可能同源于同一证据，基于报告数量的聚合器无法区分复制与独立证实。通过理论形式化和2万多次LLM智能体实验证明，集体推理应追踪证据谱系而非报告多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体AI系统虽能提升推理能力，但新增一个智能体并不等于新增一次独立观测，因为报告可能源自同一证据或共享底层模型而产生相关性，导致聚合结果在统计上不可靠。

Method: 形式化定义报告Z相对于报告R的认知Sybil扩展条件I(Θ; Z|R)=0；提出高斯共享根模型刻画共同祖先与重复提取的信息增益；设计超过20,000次受控LLM智能体报告实验，改变证据根数和报告数量验证预测，并通过受控操纵分离"表征相似性"与"证据谱系"。

Result: 报告数量从1增至32时，朴素后验覆盖率从0.940降至0.263；证据根数增至16时聚合器统计上不可区分；复制提取错误相关（γ=0.719）；表征相似性机制对聚类数影响（1.425）远大于真实谱系四倍变化（0.040）。

Conclusion: 集体推理应追踪证据谱系和依赖关系，而非智能体或报告的数量与相似性。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI systems improve inference by spawning agents and synthesizing reports. But another agent is not another observation: apparently independent reports may descend from the same evidence, and genuinely independent evidence can produce nearly identical reports. We formalize this as an epistemic Sybil problem. A report Z is an epistemic Sybil extension relative to reports R when I(Theta; Z | R) = 0. No report-only aggregator can generally distinguish replication from independent corroboration: identical reports can warrant different posteriors under unobserved ancestry. A Gaussian shared-root model shows common ancestry does not imply complete redundancy. Repeated extraction adds information toward a source-level ceiling, and correlated extraction errors, which a shared base model can induce among independent agents, lower that ceiling further. We test these predictions with more than 20,000 controlled LLM-agent report and extraction calls on synthetic evidentiary documents. Holding one evidence root fixed while report multiplicity rises from 1 to 32 collapses naive posterior coverage from 0.940 to 0.263. Holding report count fixed while evidence-root multiplicity rises from 1 to 16 closes the gap, and the aggregators are statistically indistinguishable at k = 16. The agent's replicate extraction errors are correlated (gamma_cal = 0.719, estimated out of sample), and a correlated-extraction aggregator restores calibration accordingly. A controlled manipulation isolates representation similarity from evidential ancestry. It changes a report-space deduplication mechanism's mean inferred cluster count by 1.425 (95% CI [1.363, 1.485]), whereas a fourfold change in true ancestry changes it by only 0.040 ([-0.045, 0.120]). Collective inference should therefore track evidential ancestry and dependence, not agent or report multiplicity or similarity.

</details>


### [274] [Architecting Conversational Data Systems for Stateless LLM APIs: The Hydration Proxy Pattern](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01834)
*Joseph Axisa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"水合代理模式"（Hydration Proxy Pattern）架构，用于解决LLM API无状态化带来的对话状态管理与语义记忆负担问题，并引入"上下文稳定化命令"（Context Stabilization Mandate）来平衡主权状态管理与KV缓存之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业平台向对话式推理界面转型时，LLM API的无状态特性虽然有利于AI提供商的水平扩展，但将对话状态和语义记忆的完整管理负担转移到了客户端应用，形成了架构上的鸿沟。

Method: 提出Hydration Proxy Pattern，一种将会话持久化与推理引擎解耦的架构，确保平台对对话数据的主权同时支持安全的、多阶段的语义接地；并提出Context Stabilization Mandate以解决主权状态管理与KV缓存之间的取舍问题。

Result: 论文通过架构模式与规范框架的提出，系统性地回应了无状态LLM API在会话场景下的架构缺位问题。

Conclusion: 该工作为企业在对话式LLM部署中如何兼顾平台数据主权、会话状态管理与推理效率提供了架构层面的解决方案。

Abstract: As enterprise platforms transition to conversational reasoning interfaces, the stateless nature of LLM APIs creates an architectural gap. While statelessness enables horizontal scalability for AI providers, it forces client applications to manage the entire burden of conversational state and semantic memory. The work identifies the Hydration Proxy Pattern, an architecture that decouples session persistence from the reasoning engine. The framework ensures platform sovereignty over conversational data while enabling secure, multi-stage semantic grounding. We further propose the Context Stabilization Mandate to resolve the tradeoff between sovereign state management and KV caching.

</details>


### [275] [When Agents Implement Systems: A Case Study in Defects, Detection, and Evaluation Rigor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01985)
*Phanindra Reddy Madduru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过案例研究，分析了一个LLM编码智能体在实现多组件数据系统时的行为，记录了五类缺陷，并在HotpotQA基准上评估了过滤检索策略与未过滤搜索的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM编码智能体越来越多地承担端到端工程工作，我们缺乏对其在系统级需求（如模式设计、异步编排、配置正确性、检索过滤权衡）上行为的实证刻画。

Method: 对单个LLM编码智能体实现多组件数据系统进行案例研究，存储技术和架构预先固定，智能体自主负责实现与缺陷修复；并根据预定义规范在HotpotQA基准上对图识别实体集过滤后排序与未过滤搜索两种检索策略进行对比评估。

Result: 在单次会话中识别出5类缺陷。检索实验表明，过滤检索在预算为3时达到召回上限，而即使预算为10，未过滤搜索仅能恢复69%的必要证据，该差距在每档预算下均成立（符号检验p<0.0001）。

Conclusion: 讨论了智能体自主性成功与需要纠正之处，包括一个声称的性能修复从未在触发回归的问题上重新测量的案例，揭示了LLM编码智能体在系统级工程任务中的局限与风险。

Abstract: As LLM coding agents increasingly perform end-to-end engineering work, we lack empirical characterization of how they behave on systems-level requirements: schema design, async orchestration, configuration correctness, and retrieval-filtering trade-offs. We present a case study of one such agent implementing a multi-component data system against a detailed pre-existing specification. Storage technologies, schema, entity-resolution algorithm, and retrieval-filtering strategy were fixed in advance; the agent autonomy was in the implementation, in diagnosing and fixing defects it introduced, and in interaction-design choices left open. Over a single session, we catalog five such defects, categorized by constraint violated and detection method. We further evaluate, on the public HotpotQA benchmark, the one retrieval trade-off specified in that architecture: restricting candidates to a graph-identified entity set before ranking versus unfiltered search. We substitute the benchmark gold evidence labels for entity identification, since we lacked LLM access to run that stage, and report standard recall rather than the benchmark own accuracy metrics. Across retrieval budgets from 1 to 10 and 100 questions against a pooled corpus of 2994 paragraphs, filtered recall reaches its ceiling by a budget of 3, expected once candidates are restricted to the gold paragraphs themselves, while unfiltered search recovers all required evidence only 69 percent of the time even at a budget of 10, a gap that holds at every budget tested, with sign test p less than 0.0001. We close with a discussion of where the agent autonomy succeeded versus required correction, including one instance where a claimed performance fix was never re-measured on the regression that motivated it.

</details>


### [276] [SSAKG 2.0: An Open-Source Package for Structural Associative Sequence Memory and Context-Based Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01849)
*Przemysław Stokłosa,Janusz A. Starzyk,Paweł Raif*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍SSAKG 2.0，一个用于构建和操作结构顺序关联知识图谱（SSAKG）的开源软件包，利用稀疏图作为联想记忆，可从部分无序上下文中重建完整序列，并通过C与Python混合实现提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的序列记忆与重建方法在处理大规模稀疏图时存在内存和计算开销大的问题，需要一种高效、灵活的软件工具来构建和操作结构顺序关联知识图谱。

Method: SSAKG 2.0将对象表示为图顶点，有序序列表示为图连接的结构模式；新版引入利用计算机内存单个比特来高效搜索图连接的算法；核心图操作使用C实现并通过Python接口暴露，形成混合架构。

Result: 使用随机生成的数字序列、NLTK语料库句子衍生的序列和mRNA序列进行实验评估，验证了该包能从部分上下文中存储和重建序列的能力，并揭示了图密度、序列长度和记忆大小对检索性能的影响。

Conclusion: SSAKG 2.0以Apache 2.0开源许可发布，提供文档和可复现示例，通过GitHub和PyPI公开获取，为结构顺序联想记忆研究提供了实用的开源工具。

Abstract: This article presents SSAKG 2.0, an open-source software package for constructing and operating Structural Sequential Associative Knowledge Graphs (SSAKGs). An SSAKG represents objects as graph vertices and ordered sequences as structural patterns of graph connections. The resulting sparse graph is used as an associative memory in which complete sequences can be reconstructed from a partial, unordered context.
  Version 2.0 introduces new algorithms that exploit individual bits of computer memory to efficiently search graph connections. The package is implemented in Python, while performance-critical graph operations are implemented in C and exposed through a Python interface. This hybrid implementation provides a flexible high-level programming environment while reducing the memory and computational overhead associated with large sparse graphs.
  The algorithms were evaluated using randomly generated numerical sequences, sequences derived from sentences in the NLTK corpus, and mRNA sequences. The experiments demonstrate the ability of the package to store and reconstruct sequences from partial contexts and provide a basis for evaluating the effects of graph density, sequence length, and memory size on retrieval performance.
  SSAKG 2.0 is distributed under the Apache 2.0 open-source license. The package includes documentation and reproducible examples and is publicly available through GitHub and the Python Package Index (PyPI).

</details>


### [277] [ClaimReceipt: Verifying Evidence Sufficiency and Coverage in Agent Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01992)
*Peiying Zhu,Sidi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ClaimReceipt，一种面向智能体评估的声明相对收据规范与选择性验证器，通过将类型化交易证据绑定到签名实验清单，实现对声明证据充分性与覆盖性的可靠验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体评估面临两个不同的证据问题：报告声明能否从保留证据中重新计算（充分性），以及保留记录是否覆盖承诺的实验集（覆盖性）。通用日志和哈希链接文本无法可靠回答这两个问题。

Method: ClaimReceipt引入声明相对收据规范（claim-relative receipt specification），将类型化交易证据绑定到签名实验清单，返回PASS、INVALID或INCONCLUSIVE三种结果。规范在实现前冻结（SHA-256 18d109...b81），并在1,392条历史买家-卖家记录上进行CR-2验证器测试，随后运行独立的前瞻性CR-3阶段：30项任务在推理前提交，终端收据签名并链式连接，私有证据加密供审计员使用。

Result: CR-2验证器复现了全部5个人工标注的审计判定，精确重放600条确定性记录和792条生成后记录，使13个声明字段组在消融下均非冗余，在11/11语义故障上返回预期结果且0/8误报。CR-3阶段：完整证据获得覆盖与核算PASS；扣留一个终端收据返回INCONCLUSIVE_COVERAGE；扣留所有私有开放保持覆盖和协议验证但使经济声明不确定，完全匹配预注册预测。收据插桩仅增加模型推理时间的0.021%和每笔交易9.9 KB。

Conclusion: 声明验证需要同时满足声明充分的证据和已提交的承诺宇宙（committed universe），使遗漏变得可见。此外，规范可读性探针表明冻结规范对独立读者尚非完全无歧义，仍需改进。

Abstract: Agent evaluations face two distinct evidentiary questions: whether a reported claim is recomputable from retained evidence (sufficiency), and whether the retained records cover the committed experiment set (coverage). Generic logs and hash-linked transcripts answer neither reliably. We introduce ClaimReceipt, a claim-relative receipt specification and selective verifier that binds typed transaction evidence to a signed experiment manifest and returns PASS, INVALID, or INCONCLUSIVE per claim. We freeze the specification before implementation (SHA-256 18d109...b81). On 1,392 historical buyer--seller records, a CR-2 verifier reproduces all five manually labeled audit verdicts, exactly replays 600 deterministic and 792 post-generation records, makes every one of 13 declared field groups non-redundant under tested ablations, and returns the expected result on 11/11 semantic faults with 0/8 false positives. We then run a separate prospective CR-3 epoch: 30 assignments are committed before inference, terminal receipts are signed and chained, and private evidence is encrypted for an auditor. Complete evidence yields coverage and accounting PASS; withholding one terminal receipt returns INCONCLUSIVE_COVERAGE, while withholding all private openings preserves coverage and protocol verification but makes economic claims inconclusive, exactly matching a preregistered prediction. Receipt instrumentation adds 0.021% of model-inference time and 9.9 KB per transaction. A specification-legibility probe indicates that our own frozen specification is not yet unambiguous to an independent reader. Claim verification therefore requires both claim-sufficient evidence and a committed universe against which omissions become visible.

</details>


### [278] [The Memory Trust Gap: Capability-Dependent Failures in Persistent-Memory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01852)
*Jundong Hu,Shekar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了持久记忆系统中，过时存储事实在模型能力变化时如何开始覆盖权威证据的问题。通过在Qwen3系列（0.6/1.7/4/8B）上评估冻结的闭集基准（含Benefit和Safety两个套件），发现过时记忆的过度信任（Memory Trust Gap）在模型规模上的表现存在差异：Safety套件中的伤害具有能力门控特征，较大模型更容易被伪装成"当前"的过时笔记误导；问题特征对过度信任的触发作用取决于模型规模，移除标签在所有尺寸上都放大过度信任，而源权威性薄弱且随规模平坦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久记忆支持个性化智能体，但过时的存储事实可能在没有警告的情况下覆盖当前权威证据。理解这种危害何时随模型能力变化而出现，对于设计更安全、更可靠的记忆系统至关重要。

Method: 使用一个冻结的、闭集的、动作评分的基准，包含2个套件（Benefit和Safety），在Qwen3系列（0.6/1.7/4/8B）上进行评估。采用2×2×2×2因式实验设计，并使用直接跨规模对比检验而非重叠的逐模型置信区间。还通过Llama-Instruct系列和外部数据集（RGB、MisBench）进行验证。

Result: 在Benefit套件中，所有规模模型都0.92-1.00地使用过时值。在Safety套件中，伤害在陷阱条件下表现出能力门控，较大模型崩溃更严重。移除标签在所有规模上放大过度信任，较新的过时日期更能迷惑较大模型。源权威性弱且规模平坦；位置效果随规模由正转负。缓解措施也依赖能力：暴露元数据只对大模型有效，而预解决冲突才能恢复小模型准确性。

Conclusion: 记忆过度信任随模型能力呈非均匀变化，特征（如标签和日期）的触发作用取决于规模，缓解策略需因模型能力而异。

Abstract: Persistent memory supports personalized agents, but a stale stored fact can override current authoritative evidence without warning. We study when this harm begins as model capability changes. We evaluate a frozen, closed-set, action-scored benchmark with 2 suites that represent 2 different meanings of "no memory" (a Benefit suite, unsolvable without the stored fact, and a Safety suite, in which an authoritative tool always holds the correct value), on a same-family model-size series (Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B). The Memory Trust Gap reflects over-trust rather than confusion. In the Benefit suite, models answer with the stale value 0.92-1.00 of the time at every scale. In the Safety suite, harm below the no-memory baseline under the trap conditions ($Δ_{\mathrm{mem}}$) is capability-gated, with the larger models collapsing most once a stale note is made to look current. In a $2\times2\times2\times2$ factorial, which feature triggers over-trust depends on both the feature and model scale. Removing a label amplifies over-trust at every size, and a recency feature (stale dated newer) fools the larger models harder. Source authority is weak and scale-flat, and position changes from positive to negative across the Qwen3 model-size series. We confirm these scale interactions with direct cross-size contrast tests rather than overlapping per-model intervals. Mitigation is likewise capability-dependent: exposing metadata improves accuracy for the capable models, but only pre-resolving the conflict restores accuracy for the 2 smaller checkpoints. The same pattern appears on the capable models in an independent Llama-Instruct model-size series and on 2 external datasets (RGB, MisBench). A framing control finds no consistent advantage for the memory label: at the 3 smaller scales, models trust a stale document more than a stale memory; at 8B, the difference is not significant.

</details>


### [279] [Propose to Learn, Learn to Propose: Evaluability-Aware Assistance under Bounded Rationality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02242)
*Yifan Zhu,Sammie Katt,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究AI助手中考虑用户可评估性（evaluability）的提案规划问题，提出ProSE框架和ProSE-Plan规划器，通过将提案同时作为任务干预和偏好探测手段，在用户有限理性条件下提升协助效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI协助方法通常假设用户能可靠评估任何提案，但实际中用户存在有限理性（bounded rationality），导致对提案的评估能力受限。这一被忽视的维度可能使仅追求提案采纳率的规划器系统性地表现不佳。

Method: 形式化为ProSE（隐藏参数序贯协助问题），使用KL正则化的有限理性二元响应模型（接受概率权衡价值增益与距离依赖的可评估性惩罚）；提出ProSE-Plan，一种深度为2的贝叶斯自适应规划器，通过可能响应和响应诱导的后验信念对提案打分。

Result: 在受控图模拟中，当评估成本成为瓶颈时，ProSE-Plan优于不考虑可评估性的基线和短视基线；probe-commit消融实验证实该方法能选择更简单的提案方法遗漏的信息性提案。

Conclusion: 用户可评估性是AI协助中一个与规划相关的关键维度，与生成质量和偏好推断互补；容易被接受的提案和具有信息量的探测提案不总是一致的，规划器需要同时考虑这两者。

Abstract: AI assistants often collaborate by proposing candidate edits, plans, or designs that users evaluate before adoption. Existing assistance methods focus on proposal quality or user-goal inference, often assuming that the user can reliably evaluate any proposal, which can fail in practice because of bounded rationality. We study evaluability-aware proposal planning, where proposals serve both as task interventions and as probes for learning latent preferences and evaluation constraints, where the resulting belief updates then guide later proposals. We formalise this setting as ProSE, a hidden-parameter sequential assistance problem, and instantiate it with a KL-regularised bounded-rational binary response model in which acceptance trades off value gain against a distance-dependent evaluability penalty. Analysing the planning consequence of this likelihood reveals that likely accepted proposals and informative probes need not coincide, which explains why planners that only pursue acceptance systematically underperform. We operationalise ProSE with \textsc{ProSE-Plan}, a depth-2 Bayes-adaptive planner that scores proposals by possible responses and response-induced posterior beliefs. In controlled graph simulations, \textsc{ProSE-Plan} improves over evaluability-unaware and myopic baselines when evaluation cost is the bottleneck, and a probe-commit ablation confirms that our approach selects informative proposals that simpler methods miss. Our results thus identify user evaluability as a planning-relevant dimension of AI assistance, complementary to generation quality and preference inference.

</details>


### [280] [Belief-Calibrated Optimization: An Explicit World Model for Agentic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01861)
*Yuhan Chen,Zhihua Tian,Mahavir Dabas,Charith Peris,Rahul Gupta,Ming Jin,Feiyang Kang,Siyuan Zhang,Nan Wang,Ruoxi Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出信念校准优化（BCO），一种将LLM智能体优化循环中对环境响应的隐性信念显式化为持久化上下文文档（世界模型）的方法，在五个基准上超过无世界模型的对照方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的性能依赖于冻结模型周围的脚手架（scaffold）。现有方法使用编码智能体作为优化器，它会根据分数和轨迹迭代编辑源代码，但其对环境的信念（什么出错、什么改动有效）通常是隐性的，存在于推理过程或参数中，无法在后续调用中被利用。这使得优化效率受限。

Method: BCO将隐性信念显式地写成持久化的上下文文档，随着新候选的被评估而不断修订该文档。这份文档充当世界模型，记录当前对"环境如何响应编辑"的理解。该方法加入标准的优化循环中，不改变候选选择机制。

Result: 在五个基准（记忆问答、工具使用问答、代码即行动的App智能体、终端智能体）上，BCO的训练通过率高于仅缺少世界模型的匹配对照。该差距在持久性分割（held-out split）中保持。目标模型替换后，BCO脚手架在测试任务上领先（除上下文窗口溢出情况外）。离线消融表明，差距来源于文档的内容而非仅形式——使用累积文档的预测器比无文档或伪造内容文档的预测器更准确地预测环境响应。

Conclusion: 显式记录和持续修订的信念文档（世界模型）携带可复用的信息，能有效提升LLM智能体优化器的性能，且该增益源于文档内容本身而非其形式。

Abstract: The performance of an LLM agent depends on the scaffold around a frozen model. A common way to improve that scaffold is to use a coding agent as an optimizer: it reads current scores and traces and iteratively edits the source, producing a new candidate each round. Each edit is chosen according to a belief about how the environment will respond: what went wrong, and which change should help. That belief is typically implicit. It lives in the coding agent's reasoning on the current call, or remains latent in its parameters, rather than as something written down. Later calls therefore see scores and traces, but they do not use that belief. We introduce Belief-Calibrated Optimization (BCO), a method that writes that belief down as a persistent in-context document and continually revises that document as new candidates are evaluated. The resulting document is a world model: the current account of how the environment responds to edits. Added to an otherwise standard loop, BCO reaches a higher train passrate than a matched control that lacks only the world model, on five benchmarks spanning memory QA, tool-use QA, code-as-action app agents, and terminal agents. The gap remains on every held-out split, which is not used to select the candidate. After a target-model swap, in which the frozen model is replaced and the scaffold is not, the selected BCO scaffold leads on the tasks we test, except where context-window overruns leave it unfinished. An offline ablation then asks whether that gap comes from what the world model says. A fresh predictor given the accumulated document forecasts how the environment will respond more accurately than predictors given either no document or a same-form copy whose content has been falsified. The comparison indicates that the document carries reusable information in its content, not only in its form.

</details>


### [281] [Codebook Agent: Amortized Topology Design for LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02264)
*Jinxi Yu,Yubei Li,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Zhi Zhang,Dong Liu,Wenxiao Zhao,Levina Li,Kai-Wei Chang,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文指出现有LLM多智能体系统通信拓扑设计方法与问题本质不一致，并提出了Codebook Agent方法：通过向量量化自编码器将成功拓扑压缩为固定16条码本的码本，配合奖励加权MLP和MLP代理进行拓扑生成与重排序，无需迭代搜索和消息传递，在六个基准上达到最高准确率且显著降低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者通过三个实证发现指出现有拓扑设计方法的根本问题：(1) 即使码本容量从8增至64，通过奖励过滤后存活的拓扑结构仅收敛到约6种不同图；(2) 边数与实测token消耗呈负相关（Pearson r≈-0.4），即稀疏化图反而使推理更昂贵，说明用边数作为结构代价与真实效率目标矛盾；(3) 当智能体共享配置文件时（已发表基准的默认设置），基于消息传递的评分器在图谱上具有邻接不变性，因而无法对候选拓扑排序。

Method: Codebook Agent包含三个核心组件：(1) 向量量化自编码器，将成功的拓扑压缩为查询无关的16条目码本；(2) 奖励加权MLP，将查询嵌入映射到码本上的分布；(3) 读取展平邻接矩阵的MLP代理，基于实测效用和每任务归一化的token成本对解码出的候选拓扑进行重排序，整个过程在单个批处理前向传播中完成。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上，Codebook Agent平均准确率达84.6%，超过此前最强设计器的83.0%；拓扑生成仅需2.4毫秒；与基线相比节省21.9%-33.2%的LLM token消耗。

Conclusion: 将通信拓扑生成视为条件图生成（变分/自回归/扩散+图网络代理）的现有范式与实际问题目标不匹配。Codebook Agent通过离线压缩成功拓扑为固定码本、在线轻量解码的方式，在不进行迭代搜索和消息传递的情况下，同时实现了更高的准确率、更低的延迟和更少的token消耗，验证了重新审视问题建模方向的必要性。

Abstract: Adapting the communication topology of an LLM multi-agent system to each query improves both accuracy and efficiency, yet current designers treat this as conditional graph generation: a variational, autoregressive, or diffusion decoder searches the $N \times N$ adjacency space, and a graph-network proxy trained on utility and a structural cost such as edge count ranks the sampled candidates. We argue that this formulation is misaligned with the problem. Empirically, topologies that survive a reward filter collapse to about six distinct graphs even when the codebook capacity grows from 8 to 64; edge count is negatively correlated with measured token consumption (Pearson $r \approx -0.4$), so sparsifying the graph makes inference more expensive; and a message-passing scorer over agent-profile nodes is adjacency-invariant whenever agents share a profile---the default configuration of published benchmarks---so it cannot rank candidates at all in that regime. These three facts motivate Codebook Agent: a vector-quantized autoencoder compresses successful topologies into a query-independent 16-entry codebook; a reward-weighted MLP maps the query embedding to a distribution over codes; and an MLP proxy that reads the flattened adjacency, regressed on measured utility and per-task normalized token cost, reranks the top decoded candidates in a single batched forward pass. With no iterative search and no message passing at test time, Codebook Agent is the most accurate method on all six benchmarks we compare (84.6 average against 83.0 for the strongest prior designer), emits a topology in 2.4 ms, and uses 21.9--33.2% fewer LLM tokens.

</details>


### [282] [Collective creativity in hybrid societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02620)
*Mason Youngblood,Katie Mudd,Manuel Anglada-Tort,Cameron Jones,Elena Miu,Diana Omigie,Margaret Schedel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨生成式AI对创造力的影响，提出创造力应分为新颖性（单一作品的属性）和多样性（群体的属性），并主张在生成式AI背景下，创造力应被视为人类与算法混合群体的属性，而非个体的属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者对生成式AI是丰富还是削弱文化存在分歧，作者认为分歧源于混淆了创造力的两个不同维度：新颖性与多样性。

Method: 通过概念辨析与理论论证，区分创造力的新颖性（个体层面）与多样性（群体层面）两个成分，论证创造力应被视为混合集体（人类与算法交互的种群）的属性，并分析群体组成（哪些代理在场、比例如何、连接方式）对结果的决定性影响。

Result: AI辅助构思可靠地提升个体输出新颖性，但会在总体上收窄多样性。然而这并非必然结果——由于人类与模型的搜索方式互补，混合群体可以在表现和多样性上超越任何单一类型群体。

Conclusion: 结论是问题不再是AI是否帮助或损害创造力，而是何种混合组合能让个体收益累积而不侵蚀集体多样性。

Abstract: Generative AI is changing how cultural artifacts are created and circulated, and with it our understanding of creativity itself. Researchers disagree about whether these tools enrich or impoverish culture, and we argue that much of that disagreement comes from conflating two distinct components of creativity: novelty, a property of single artifacts, and diversity, a property of populations. We argue further that creativity in the context of generative AI is best understood as a property of hybrid collectives, or populations of interacting people and algorithms, rather than of individuals.
